## 追蹤 (追蹤網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　93da91fc4e396cfd18-14

**english**　Trace

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 追蹤網絡工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html

**parameter**　condition_barriers

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 條件障礙 （可選）
- 基于與網絡屬性的比較，對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙條件。 條件障礙使用網絡屬性、運算符和類型以及屬性值。 例如，當圖徵的 Code 屬性等于 ArtificialPath 這一特定值時，將停止追蹤。 當圖徵滿足此條件時，追蹤將停止。 如果您要使用多個屬性，可使用組合方法參數來定義 And 或 Or 條件。 值表列： Name—Filter by network attribute name. Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Type—Filter by a specific type. Specific value—Filter by a specific value. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Filter by a specific value. Combine Using—Combine multiple condition barriers. And—Combine the condition barriers. Or—Use if either condition barrier is met.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#parameters

**id**　93da91fc4e396cfd18-15

**english**　Trace

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 追蹤網絡工具箱

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#parameters

**parameter**　function_barriers

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 函數障礙 （可選）
- 基于函數對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙。 函數障礙可用于執行以下操作：限制追蹤距離起點的行程或設置停止追蹤的最大值。 例如，所經過的每條線的長度和為目前經過的總距離。 當經過的總長度達到指定值時，追蹤將停止。 值表列： Function—Filter by a function. Min—The minimum of the input values. Max—The maximum of the input values. Average—The average of the input values. Count—The number of features. Add—Add the values. Subtract—Subtract the values. Attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Value—Filter by a specific value. Use Local Values—Boolean value to use local variables instead of a global variable.
- Value Table

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#parameters

**english**　Trace

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html

**parameter**　traversability_scope

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 應用可遍歷性至 （可選）
- 指定將應用的可遍歷性類型。 可遍歷性范圍用于確定是否在交匯點和/或邊處應用可遍歷性。 例如，在游憩步道網絡中，如果條件障礙定義為當 SurfaceType 屬性包含值“gravel”并且可遍歷性范圍設置為僅交匯點時停止追蹤，則即使遇到 Surfacetype 欄位具有此值的邊圖徵，追蹤也不會停止，因為 Surfacetype 屬性僅適用于邊。 交匯點和邊—可遍歷性將同時應用于交匯點和邊。 這是默認設置。 僅交匯點—可遍歷性將僅應用于交匯點。 僅邊—可遍歷性將僅應用于邊。
- String

**source_language**　zh-cn

**parameter**　output_conditions

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 輸出條件 （可選）
- 根據網絡屬性返回的圖徵類型。 例如，如果將追蹤配置為“過濾掉除水龍頭圖徵外的所有圖徵”，那么未分配“水龍頭”屬性的追蹤圖徵均不會包含在結果中。 任何追蹤的圖徵都會返回到結果選擇集中。 值表列： Name—Filter by network attribute name. Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Type—Filter by a specific type. Specific value—Filter by a specific value. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Filter by a specific value. Combine Using—Combine multiple conditions. And—Combine the conditions. Or—Use if either condition is met.
- Value Table

## 追蹤 (追蹤網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　93da91fc4e396cfd18-19

**english**　Trace

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 追蹤網絡工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html

**parameter**　result_types

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 結果類型 （可選）
- 指定追蹤返回的結果的類型。 選擇—追蹤結果將作為相應網絡圖徵上的選擇集返回。 這是默認設置。 聚合幾何—追蹤結果按幾何類型聚合，并存儲在活動地圖的圖層中顯示的圖徵類中。 網絡圖層—追蹤結果將被作為圖層組內的選擇集添加到圖徵圖層。 連通性—追蹤結果將作為指定輸出 .json 文件中的連通圖返回。 此選項將啟用輸出 JSON 參數。 元素—追蹤結果將作為指定輸出 .json 文件中的基于圖徵的信息返回。 此選項將啟用輸出 JSON 參數。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#parameters

## 追蹤 (追蹤網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　93da91fc4e396cfd18-20

**english**　Trace

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 追蹤網絡工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html

**parameter**　selection_type

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 選擇內容類型
- 指定如何應用所選內容以及如果已存在當前內容要執行的操作。 新建選擇內容—生成的選擇內容將替換當前選擇內容。 這是默認設置。 添加到當前選擇內容—當存在一個選擇內容時，會將生成的選擇內容添加到當前選擇內容中。 如果不存在選擇內容，該選項的作用與新選擇內容選項的作用相同。 從當前選擇內容中移除—將生成的選擇內容從當前選擇內容中移除。 如果不存在選擇內容，該選項不起作用。 選擇當前選擇內容的子集—將生成的選擇內容與當前選擇內容進行組合。 僅兩者共有的記錄保持選中狀態。 切換當前選擇內容—生成的選擇內容將被切換。 將所選的結果從當前選擇內容中移除，同時將未選取的結果添加到當前選擇內容中。 如果不存在選擇內容，該選項的作用與新選擇內容選項的作用相同。
- String

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#parameters

- trace_type
- 指定要執行的追蹤的類型。 CONNECTED—將使用從一個或多個起點開始并沿著已連接圖徵向外跨越的已連接追蹤。 UPSTREAM—將使用能夠發現網絡中某位置上游的圖徵的上溯追蹤。 這種類型的追蹤需要設置流向。 DOWNSTREAM—將使用能夠發現網絡中某位置下游的圖徵的下溯追蹤。 這種類型的追蹤需要設置流向。 SHORTEST_PATH—將使用可查找網絡中兩個起點之間的最短路徑（不考慮流向）的最短路徑追蹤。 遍歷路徑的成本取決于為 shortest_path_network_attribute_name 參數設置的網絡屬性。
- String

- 使用追蹤位置窗格中的起點選項卡創建的圖徵圖層，或包含一個或多個表示追蹤起點的記錄的表或圖徵類。 在使用追蹤位置窗格中的起點工具創建起點時，默認情況下，將使用 TN_Temp_Starting_Points 圖徵類，并在工程的默認地理資料庫中生成該圖徵類。

**interface**　python

- 資料類型

**values**　
- validate_consistency （可選）
- 指定如果在任何遍歷的圖徵中遇到臟區是否返回錯誤。 這是保證追蹤通過網絡中狀態一致圖徵的唯一方法。 要移除臟區，請驗證網絡拓撲。 VALIDATE_CONSISTENCY—如果在任何遍歷的圖徵中遇到臟區，追蹤將返回錯誤。 這是默認設置。 DO_NOT_VALIDATE_CONSISTENCY—無論是否在遍歷的圖徵中遇到臟區，追蹤都將返回結果。

## 追蹤 (追蹤網絡工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　93da91fc4e396cfd18-41

**english**　Trace

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 追蹤網絡工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html

**parameter**　condition_barriers

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- condition_barriers [Name, Operator, Type, Value, Combine Using,...] （可選）
- 基于與網絡屬性的比較，對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙條件。 條件障礙使用網絡屬性、運算符和類型以及屬性值。 例如，當圖徵的 Code 屬性等于 ArtificialPath 這一特定值時，將停止追蹤。 當圖徵滿足此條件時，追蹤將停止。 如果要使用多個屬性，可使用 Combine Using 組件來定義 And 或 Or 條件。 值表列： Name—Filter by network attribute name. Operator—Filter by an operator. IS_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is equal to the value. DOES_NOT_EQUAL—The attribute is not equal to the value. IS_GREATER_THAN—The attribute is greater than the value. IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. IS_LESS_THAN—The attribute is less than the value. IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Type—Filter by a specific type. SPECIFIC_VALUE—Filter by a specific value. NETWORK_ATTRIBUTE—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Filter by a specific value. Combine Using—Combine multiple condition barriers. AND—Combine the condition barriers. OR—Use if either condition barrier is met.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#derived-output

## 追蹤 (追蹤網絡工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　93da91fc4e396cfd18-42

**english**　Trace

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 追蹤網絡工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html

**parameter**　function_barriers

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- function_barriers [Function, Attribute, Operator, Value, Use Local Values,...] （可選）
- 基于函數對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙。 函數障礙可用于執行以下操作：限制追蹤距離起點的行程或設置停止追蹤的最大值。 例如，所經過的每條線的長度和為目前經過的總距離。 當經過的總長度達到指定值時，追蹤將停止。 值表列： Function—Filter by a function. MIN—The minimum of the input values. MAX—The maximum of the input values. AVERAGE—The average of the input values. COUNT—The number of features. ADD—Add the values. SUBTRACT—Subtract the values. Attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Operator—Filter by an operator. IS_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is equal to the value. DOES_NOT_EQUAL—The attribute is not equal to the value. IS_GREATER_THAN—The attribute is greater than the value. IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. IS_LESS_THAN—The attribute is less than the value. IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Value—Filter by a specific value. Use Local Values—Boolean value to use local variables instead of a global variable.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#derived-output

**parameter**　traversability_scope

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- traversability_scope （可選）
- 指定將應用的可遍歷性類型。 可遍歷性范圍用于確定是否在交匯點和/或邊處應用可遍歷性。 例如，在游憩步道網絡中，如果條件障礙定義為當路徑類型為“礫石”并且可遍歷性范圍設置為僅交匯點時停止追蹤，則即使遇到礫石路徑，追蹤也不會停止，因為該路徑類型僅適用于邊。 BOTH_JUNCTIONS_AND_EDGES—可遍歷性將同時應用于交匯點和邊。 這是默認設置。 JUNCTIONS_ONLY—可遍歷性將僅應用于交匯點。 EDGES_ONLY—可遍歷性將僅應用于邊。
- String

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#derived-output

**values**　
- selection_type
- 指定如何應用所選內容以及如果已存在當前內容要執行的操作。 NEW_SELECTION—生成的選擇內容將替換當前選擇內容。 這是默認設置。 ADD_TO_SELECTION—當存在一個選擇內容時，會將生成的選擇內容添加到當前選擇內容中。 如果不存在選擇內容，該選項的作用與新選擇內容選項的作用相同。 REMOVE_FROM_SELECTION—將生成的選擇內容從當前選擇內容中移除。 如果不存在選擇內容，該選項不起作用。 SUBSET_SELECTION—將生成的選擇內容與當前選擇內容進行組合。 僅兩者共有的記錄保持選中狀態。 SWITCH_SELECTION—生成的選擇內容將被切換。 將所選的結果從當前選擇內容中移除，同時將未選取的結果添加到當前選擇內容中。 如果不存在選擇內容，該選項的作用與新選擇內容選項的作用相同。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#derived-output

## 追蹤 (追蹤網絡工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　93da91fc4e396cfd18-58

**english**　Trace

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 追蹤網絡工具箱

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html

**text**　'''****************************************************************************
Name:        UpstreamTrace.py
Description: This script runs an upstream trace in a trace network from a 
             specified starting point to return the total length of the 
             features using the network layers result type.
Created by:  Esri
****************************************************************************'''

# Import required modules
import arcpy

# Set local variables
in_trace_network = "Hydro Network"
trace_type = "UPSTREAM"
starting_points = "C:\\MyProject\\HydroTN.gdb\\TN_Temp_Starting_Points"
barriers = "C:\\MyProject\\HydroTN.gdb\\TN_Temp_Barriers"
path_direction = "NO_DIRECTION"
include_barriers = "INCLUDE_BARRIERS"
functions = "ADD 'Shape length' # # # # 'Total Length (Meters)'"
result_types = "NETWORK_LAYERS"
out_network_layer = "My_Trace_Result"

# Run Trace with specified parameters and leave the rest default
arcpy.tn.Trace(in_trace_network,
               trace_type,
               starting_points,
               barriers,
               path_direction,
               include_barriers,
               functions,
               result_types,
               out_network_layer)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#derived-output

## 追蹤 (追蹤網絡工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 追蹤網絡工具箱

**english**　Trace

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html

**id**　93da91fc4e396cfd18-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Trace，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入追蹤網絡 [in_trace_network]、追蹤類型 [trace_type]、起點 [starting_points]、障礙 （可選） [barriers]、路徑方向 （可選） [path_direction]、最短路徑網絡屬性名稱 （可選） [shortest_path_network_attribute_name]、包括障礙圖徵 （可選） [include_barriers]、驗證一致性 （可選） [validate_consistency]、忽略起點處障礙 （可選） [ignore_barriers_at_starting_points]、允許不確定的流 （可選） [allow_indeterminate_flow]、條件障礙 （可選） [condition_barriers]、函數障礙 （可選） [function_barriers]、應用可遍歷性至 （可選） [traversability_scope]、函數 （可選） [functions]、輸出條件 （可選） [output_conditions]、結果類型 （可選） [result_types]、選擇內容類型 [selection_type]、清除所有之前的追蹤結果 （可選） [clear_all_previous_trace_results]、追蹤名稱 （可選） [trace_name]、聚合的點 （可選） [aggregated_points]、聚合的線 （可選） [aggregated_lines]、輸出圖層組名稱 （可選） [out_network_layer]、使用追蹤配置 （可選） [use_trace_config]、追蹤配置名稱 （可選） [trace_config_name]、輸出 JSON （可選） [out_json_file]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/trace-network/trace.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_trace_network", "label_zh": "輸入追蹤網絡", "label_en": "Input Trace Network", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Trace Network; Trace Network Layer", "description_en": "The trace network on which the trace will be run. When working with an enterprise geodatabase, an input trace network must be from a feature service; a trace network from a database connection is not supported."}, {"parameter": "trace_type", "label_zh": "追蹤類型", "label_en": "Trace Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of trace to execute. Connected—A connected trace that begins at one or more starting points and spans outward along connected features will be used. Upstream—An upstream trace that discovers features upstream from a location in the network will be used. This type of trace requires the flow direction to be set. Downstream—A downstream trace that discovers features downstream from a location in the network will be used. This type of trace requires the flow direction to be set. Shortest path—A shortest path trace that finds the shortest path between two starting points in the network regardless of flow direction will be used. The cost of traversing the path is determined based on the network attribute set for the Shortest Path Network Attribute Name parameter. Connected—A connected trace that begins at one or more starting points and spans outward along connected features will be used. Upstream—An upstream trace that discovers features upstream from a location in the network will be used. This type of trace requires the flow direction to be set. Downstream—A downstream trace that discovers features downstream from a location in the network will be used. This type of trace requires the flow direction to be set. Shortest path—A shortest path trace that finds the shortest path between two starting points in the network regardless of flow direction will be used. The cost of traversing the path is determined based on the network attribute set for the shortest_path_network_attribute_name parameter."}, {"parameter": "starting_points", "label_zh": "起點", "label_en": "Starting Points", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer; Table View", "description_en": "A feature layer created using the Starting Points tab in the Trace Locations pane, or a table or feature class containing one or more records that represent the starting points of the trace. The TN_Temp_Starting_Points feature class is used by default and is generated in the default geodatabase of the project when you create starting points using the Starting Points tool in the Trace Locations pane."}, {"parameter": "barriers", "label_zh": "障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer; Table View", "description_en": "A table or feature class containing one or more features that represent the barriers of the trace that prevent the trace from traversing beyond that point. The TN_Temp_Barriers feature class is used by default and is generated in the default geodatabase of the project when you create barriers using the Barriers tab in the Trace Locations pane."}, {"parameter": "path_direction", "label_zh": "路徑方向 （可選）", "label_en": "Path Direction (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the direction of the trace path. The cost of traversing the path is determined by the Shortest Path Network Attribute Name parameter value. This parameter is only honored when running a Shortest path trace type. No direction—The path between the two starting points, regardless of the direction of flow, will be used. This is the default. Upstream path—The upstream path between the two starting points will be used. Downstream path—The downstream path between the two starting points will be used."}, {"parameter": "shortest_path_network_attribute_name", "label_zh": "最短路徑網絡屬性名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Shortest Path Network Attribute Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the network attribute used to calculate the path. When running a shortest path trace type, the shortest path is calculated using a numeric network attribute such as shape length. Cost and distance based paths can both be achieved. This parameter is required when running a shortest path trace."}, {"parameter": "include_barriers", "label_zh": "包括障礙圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Include barrier features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the traversability barrier features will be included in the trace results. Checked—Traversability barrier features will be included in the trace results. This is the default. Unchecked—Traversability barrier features will not be included in the trace results."}, {"parameter": "validate_consistency", "label_zh": "驗證一致性 （可選）", "label_en": "Validate consistency (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether an error will be returned if dirty areas are encountered for any of the returned features. This is the only way to guarantee a trace is passing through features with consistent status in the network. To remove dirty areas, validate the network topology. Checked—The trace will return an error if any of the trace result has corresponding dirty areas, and select the corresponding features for you to investigate or validate. Unchecked—The trace will return results regardless of whether the trace result has corresponding dirty areas. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "ignore_barriers_at_starting_points", "label_zh": "忽略起點處障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Ignore barriers at starting points (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether barriers in the trace configuration will be ignored for starting points. Checked—Barriers at starting points will be ignored in the trace. Unchecked—Barriers at starting points will not be ignored in the trace. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "allow_indeterminate_flow", "label_zh": "允許不確定的流 （可選）", "label_en": "Allow indeterminate flow (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether features with indeterminate or uninitialized flow will be traced. This parameter is only honored when running an upstream or downstream trace. Checked—Features with indeterminate or uninitialized flow direction will be traced. Unchecked—Features with indeterminate or uninitialized flow direction will not be traced. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "condition_barriers", "label_zh": "條件障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Condition Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Sets a traversability barrier condition on features based on a comparison to a network attribute. A condition barrier uses a network attribute, an operator and a type, and an attribute value. For example, stop a trace when a feature has the Code attribute equal to the specific value of ArtificialPath. When a feature meets this condition, the trace stops. If you're using more than one attribute, you can use the Combine Using parameter to define an And or an Or condition. Value table columns: Name—Filter by network attribute name. Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Type—Filter by a specific type. Specific value—Filter by a specific value. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Filter by a specific value. Combine Using—Combine multiple condition barriers. And—Combine the condition barriers. Or—Use if either condition barrier is met."}, {"parameter": "function_barriers", "label_zh": "函數障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Function Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Sets a traversability barrier on features based on a function. Function barriers can be used, for example, to restrict how far the trace travels from the starting point or to set a maximum value to stop a trace. For example, the length of each line traveled is added to the total distance traveled so far. When the total length traveled reaches the value specified, the trace stops. Value table columns: Function—Filter by a function. Min—The minimum of the input values. Max—The maximum of the input values. Average—The average of the input values. Count—The number of features. Add—Add the values. Subtract—Subtract the values. Attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Value—Filter by a specific value. Use local values—Boolean value to use local variables instead of a global variable."}, {"parameter": "traversability_scope", "label_zh": "應用可遍歷性至 （可選）", "label_en": "Apply Traversability To (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of traversability that will be applied. Traversability scope determines whether traversability is applied to junctions, edges, or both. For example, in a network of recreational trails, if a condition barrier is defined to stop a trace if a surfacetype attribute contains a value of gravel and the traversability scope is set to junctions only, the trace will not stop even if it encounters an edge feature with this value in the surfacetype field, because the surfacetype attribute is only applicable to edges. Both junctions and edges—Traversability will be applied to both junctions and edges. This is the default. Junctions only—Traversability will be applied to junctions only. Edges only—Traversability will be applied to edges only."}, {"parameter": "functions", "label_zh": "函數 （可選）", "label_en": "Functions (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The calculation function or functions that will be applied to the trace results. Value table columns: Function—Filter by a function. Min—The minimum of the input values. Max—The maximum of the input values. Average—The average of the input values. Count—The number of features. Add—The sum of the values. Subtract—The difference between the values. Attribute—Filter by a network attribute Filter Name—Filter by attribute name. Filter Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Filter Type—Filter by attribute type. Specific value—Filter by a specific value. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Filter Value—Filter by a specific value. Function Name—The name for the alias to describe the function result."}, {"parameter": "output_conditions", "label_zh": "輸出條件 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Conditions (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The types of features that will be returned based on a network attribute. For example, in a trace configured to filter out everything but Tap features, any traced features that do not have the Tap attribute assigned to them are not included in the results. Any traced features that do are returned in the result selection set. Value table columns: Name—Filter by network attribute name. Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Type—Filter by a specific type. Specific value—Filter by a specific value. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Filter by a specific value. Combine Using—Combine multiple conditions. And—Combine the conditions. Or—Use if either condition is met."}, {"parameter": "result_types", "label_zh": "結果類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Result Types (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of results that will be returned by the trace. Selection—The trace results will be returned as a selection set on the appropriate network features. This is the default. Aggregated Geometry—The trace results will be aggregated by geometry type and stored in feature classes displayed in layers in the active map. Network Layers—The trace results will be added to feature layers as a selection set in a group layer. Connectivity—The trace results will be returned as a connectivity graph in a specified output .json file. This option enables the Output JSON parameter. Elements—The trace results will be returned as feature-based information in a specified output .json file. This option enables the Output JSON parameter."}, {"parameter": "selection_type", "label_zh": "選擇內容類型", "label_en": "Selection Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies how the selection will be applied and what to do if a current selection exists. New selection—The resulting selection will replace the current selection. This is the default. Add to the current selection—The resulting selection will be added to the current selection if one exists. If no selection exists, this is the same as the new selection option. Remove from the current selection—The resulting selection will be removed from the current selection. If no selection exists, this option has no effect. Select subset from the current selection—The resulting selection will be combined with the current selection. Only records that are common to both remain selected. Switch the current selection—The resulting selection will be switched. Results that were selected are removed from the current selection, while results that were not selected are added to the current selection. If no selection exists, this is the same as the new selection option."}, {"parameter": "clear_all_previous_trace_results", "label_zh": "清除所有之前的追蹤結果 （可選）", "label_en": "Clear all previous trace results (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether content will be truncated from or appended to the feature classes chosen to store aggregated geometry. This parameter is only applicable to the aggregated geometry result type. Checked—The feature classes storing aggregated trace geometry will be truncated. Only the output geometry from the current trace operation will be written. This is the default. Unchecked—The output geometry from the current trace operation will be appended to the feature classes storing aggregated geometry."}, {"parameter": "trace_name", "label_zh": "追蹤名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Trace Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the trace operation. This value is stored in the TRACENAME field of the output feature class to assist with identification of the trace results. This parameter is only applicable to the aggregated geometry result type."}, {"parameter": "aggregated_points", "label_zh": "聚合的點 （可選）", "label_en": "Aggregated Points (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "An output multipoint feature class that contains the aggregated result geometry. By default, the parameter value will be Trace_Results_Aggregated_Points stored in the project's default geodatabase. The tool creates this feature class if it does not exist. You can also use an existing feature class to store the aggregated geometry. If a feature class other than the default is used, it must be a point feature class and contain a string field named TRACENAME. To exclude an aggregated point layer from the trace results, clear the parameter's default value. This parameter is only applicable to the Result Types parameter's Aggregated Geometry option."}, {"parameter": "aggregated_lines", "label_zh": "聚合的線 （可選）", "label_en": "Aggregated Lines (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "An output polyline feature class that contains the aggregated result geometry. By default, the parameter value will be Trace_Results_Aggregated_Lines stored in the project's default geodatabase. The tool creates this feature class if it does not exist. You can also use an existing feature class to store the aggregated geometry. If a feature class other than the default is used, it must be a polyline feature class and contain a string field named TRACENAME. To exclude an aggregated line layer from the trace results, remove the default value from the Aggregated Lines parameter. This parameter is only applicable to the Result Types parameter's Aggregated Geometry option."}, {"parameter": "out_network_layer", "label_zh": "輸出圖層組名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Group Layer Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Group Layer", "description_en": "The name of the output group layer that contains feature layers with selection sets of features returned by the trace. The layer provides access to work with the output of a trace in ModelBuilder and Python. This parameter is only applicable to the network layers result type."}, {"parameter": "use_trace_config", "label_zh": "使用追蹤配置 （可選）", "label_en": "Use trace configuration (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether an existing named trace configuration will be used to populate the parameters of the Trace tool. Checked—An existing named trace configuration will be used to define the properties of the trace. All parameters except Trace Configuration Name, Starting Points, and Barriers will be ignored. Unchecked—An existing named trace configuration will not be used to define the properties of the trace. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "trace_config_name", "label_zh": "追蹤配置名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Trace Configuration Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the trace configuration that will be used to define the properties of the trace. This parameter is only active when the Use Trace Configuration parameter is checked."}, {"parameter": "out_json_file", "label_zh": "輸出 JSON （可選）", "label_en": "Output JSON (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "File", "description_en": "The name and location of the .json file that will be generated."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.tn.Trace(in_trace_network, {trace_type}, starting_points, {barriers}, {path_direction}, {shortest_path_network_attribute_name}, {include_barriers}, {validate_consistency}, {ignore_barriers_at_starting_points}, {allow_indeterminate_flow}, {condition_barriers}, {function_barriers}, {traversability_scope}, {functions}, {output_conditions}, {result_types}, {selection_type}, {clear_all_previous_trace_results}, {trace_name}, {aggregated_points}, {aggregated_lines}, {out_network_layer}, {use_trace_config}, {trace_config_name}, {out_json_file})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_trace_network、trace_type、starting_points、barriers、path_direction、shortest_path_network_attribute_name、include_barriers、validate_consistency、ignore_barriers_at_starting_points、allow_indeterminate_flow、condition_barriers、function_barriers、traversability_scope、functions、output_conditions、result_types、selection_type、clear_all_previous_trace_results、trace_name、aggregated_points、aggregated_lines、out_network_layer、use_trace_config、trace_config_name、out_json_file。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 鄰域選擇 (地統計分析工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　93dc09115043d27b92-26

**english**　Neighborhood Selection

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地統計分析工具箱
- 實用工具工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/neighborhood-selection.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/neighborhood-selection.html

**text**　# Name: NeighborhoodSelection_Example_02.py
# Description: Creates a layer of points based on a user-defined neighborhood.
# Requirements: Geostatistical Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/gapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inPoints = "ca_ozone_pts.shp"
outLayer = "outNS"
pointCoord = "-2000000 -50000"
maxNeighbors = 20
minNeighbors = 5
majSemiaxis = 200000
minSemiaxis = 200000
angle = 0
shape = "One Sector"

# Run NeighborhoodSelection
arcpy.ga.GANeighborhoodSelection(inPoints, outLayer, pointCoord, maxNeighbors,
                                 minNeighbors, majSemiaxis, minSemiaxis, angle,
                                 shape)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/neighborhood-selection.html#parameters

## 鄰域選擇 (地統計分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地統計分析工具箱
- 實用工具工具集

**english**　Neighborhood Selection

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/neighborhood-selection.html

**id**　93dc09115043d27b92-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/neighborhood-selection.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Neighborhood Selection，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入點圖徵 [in_dataset]、輸出圖層 [out_layer]、輸入點 [point_coord]、要包括的最大鄰域 [neighbors_max]、至少包括 [neighbors_min]、短半軸 [minor_semiaxis]、長半軸 [major_semiaxis]、角度 [angle]、形狀類型 （可選） [shape_type]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geostatistical-analyst/neighborhood-selection.html#parameters

**text**　Generates band features that represent elevation levels on a map product. The tool can be run with set values from standardized product specifications or with custom-defined values.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html#summary

## Generate Elevation Bands From Features (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　93dfd8c380e071e7e1-2

**english**　Generate Elevation Bands From Features

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 波段工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html

**text**　The Area of Interest layer must have only one feature selected.
The Area of Interest parameter value should be rectangle or square geometry. The tool may create incorrect bands if geometry other than rectangle or square is used.
Use a Contour Features parameter value that has been collected using meters or feet.
The Output Feature Class parameter value will contain the following fields: Low_Value High_Value TintBand If the feature class already exists, ensure that these fields do too.
ArcGIS Pro JOG, TPC, and ONC database product files have been updated with the appropriate fields. The BM datasets are JOG_BANDS_A, TPC_BANDS_A, and ONC_BANDS_A, respectively.
You can specify a Band Interval parameter value to create bands at regular intervals, such as every 500 meters, or you can specify irregular intervals, but not both. If a Band Interval parameter value is specified, the Band Values parameter will be inactive.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html#usage

## Generate Elevation Bands From Features (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　93dfd8c380e071e7e1-3

**english**　Generate Elevation Bands From Features

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 波段工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html

**text**　If a standard product such as JOG-A, TPC, or ONC is specified for the Product parameter, the standard ranges defined by the product specification will be automatically populated for the Band Values parameter. Bands can be added or removed as required.
If Custom is specified for the Product parameter, the Band Values parameter values must also be provided.
If a band interval is specified, the resulting band polygons will be created based on the interval, starting with 0. For example, if the input contours are in meters and the value for the Band Interval parameter is 500, the output bands will be created from 0 to 500 meters, then 500 to 1,000 meters, and so on.
If the band values specified do not fully cover the range of elevations in the selected Area of Interest (AOI), no bands will be created over those areas. For example, if Band 1 is defined as 500 to 750 meters and Band 2 is defined as 750 to 1250 meters, but the elevation range in the specified AOI is 300 to 1500 meters, no elevation bands will be created for contour-bounded areas less than 500 meters or greater than 1250 meters.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html#usage

## Generate Elevation Bands From Features (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　93dfd8c380e071e7e1-4

**english**　Generate Elevation Bands From Features

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 波段工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html

**text**　The specified Band Interval parameter value or the resulting interval from specifying Low and High values for the Band Values parameter, must be divisible by the contour interval to ensure that the Output Feature Class parameter value isn't missing bands.
The generated bands will cover the extent of both the Contour Features and Exclusion Features parameter values to avoid gaps. If the Exclusion Features parameter value is not specified, the extent of the contour lines will set the bounds of the generated bands.
Ensure that contours cover the entire AOI specified in the Area of Interest parameter. Open contour lines must extend up to or beyond the specified AOI.
Remove any intersecting contour lines from the Contour Features parameter value before running the tool; otherwise, the tool will report an error.
The contour lines specified for the Contour Features parameter should be continuous. Use the Merge Lines By Pseudo Node tool to merge line features with matching attribute values.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html#usage

- The low and high values in the bands that will be created. These values will be populated automatically from an .xml file if a particular product is specified for the product parameter value; however, these values must be provided manually if Custom is specified for the product parameter value and at least one pair of Low and High values is required. If no product is specified, at least one pair of Low and High values is still required. 值表列： Low—The lowest value in the band to be created. High—The highest value in the band to be created.
- Value Table

**text**　GenerateElevationBandsFromFeatures example (stand-alone script)
The following code sample demonstrates how to use the GenerateElevationBandsFromFeatures function.

**source_language**　zh-cn

## Generate Elevation Bands From Features (Topographic Production Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　93dfd8c380e071e7e1-24

**english**　Generate Elevation Bands From Features

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 波段工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html

**text**　# Name: GenerateElevationBandsFromFeatures_sample.py
# Description: Generates band features used to represent different elevation levels on a JOG-A product.

# Import System Modules
import arcpy

# Setting the environment
arcpy.env.overwriteOutput = True

# Make Feature Layer for Area of Interest
arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(r'C:\Users\Public\Documents\ArcGIS Topographic Mapping\Product Files\2.9\ReferenceData\MapIndex.gdb\Reference\GRID_1_DEGREE', 'JOG_Layer')

# Select Area of Interest Feature
arcpy.management.SelectLayerByAttribute('JOG_Layer', 'NEW_SELECTION', "CID = '34N120W'")

# Setting Local Variables
contour_features = r'C:\Data\JOG.gdb\BM\HypsographyCrv'
elevation_field = 'ZVH'
area_of_interest = 'JOG_Layer'
out_feature_class = r'C:\Data\JOG.gdb\BM\JOG_BANDS_A'
exclusion_features = r'C:\Data\JOG.gdb\BM\HydrographySrf'
product = 'JOG-A'
band_interval = ''
band_values = '0 1315;1315 1970;1970 5250;5250 9190;9190 12470;12470 29200'

# Use the Generate Elevation Bands From Features tool to generate band features for JOG-A product
arcpy.topographic.GenerateElevationBandsFromFeatures(contour_features, elevation_field, area_of_interest, out_feature_class, exclusion_features, product, band_interval, band_values)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html#parameters

## Generate Elevation Bands From Features (Topographic Production Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 波段工具集

**english**　Generate Elevation Bands From Features

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html

**id**　93dfd8c380e071e7e1-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/generate-elevation-bands-from-features.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Generate Elevation Bands From Features，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Contour Features [contour_features]、Elevation Field [elevation_field]、Area of Interest [area_of_interest]、Output Feature Class [out_feature_class]、Exclusion Features （可選） [exclusion_features]、Product （可選） [product]、Band Interval （可選） [band_interval]、Band Values （可選） [band_values]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

## 160638：值與同編碼值屬性域關聯的欄位類型不兼容。 · 注:

**id**　93e75fbff086e53e31-2

**english**　160638: The value is not compatible with the field types associated with the coded value domain.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 160601-160700

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160626-160650-160638.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160626-160650-160638.html

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 計算面的主角度 (制圖工具) · 匯總

**kind**　說明

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/calculate-polygon-main-angle.html

**text**　用于計算輸入面圖徵的主角度并將值分配給欄位以用于定向符號系統，例如面內的標記或影線。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/calculate-polygon-main-angle.html#summary

## 計算面的主角度 (制圖工具) · 用法

**id**　93ea409c1bf1896927-2

**english**　Calculate Polygon Main Angle

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 制圖優化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/calculate-polygon-main-angle.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/calculate-polygon-main-angle.html

**text**　面的主角度是指一組具有相似方向且總長度最長的線段的角度。 出于符號旋轉的目的，當主角度為水平時，會將面視為垂直定向。 將記錄垂直于面主角度的角度，因為這會在使用符號旋轉的值時形成預期方向。
從旋轉方法的原點開始，以十進制度將垂直于面主角度的角度存儲在指定欄位中。
此工具主要適用于正交面，而非有機形狀面。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/calculate-polygon-main-angle.html#usage

## 計算面的主角度 (制圖工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　93ea409c1bf1896927-16

**english**　Calculate Polygon Main Angle

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 制圖優化工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/calculate-polygon-main-angle.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/calculate-polygon-main-angle.html

**text**　# Name: CalculatePolygonMainAngle_standalone_script.py
# Description: Calculates the dominant angles of input polygon features and
#              assigns the values to a specified field in the feature class

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/data"

# Set local variables
in_features = "cartography.gdb/buildings_area"
angle_field = "poly_angle"
rotation_method = "ARITHMETIC"

# Run Calculate Polygon Main Angle
arcpy.cartography.CalculatePolygonMainAngle(in_features, angle_field,
                                            rotation_method)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/calculate-polygon-main-angle.html#derived-output

## 計算面的主角度 (制圖工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 制圖優化工具集

**english**　Calculate Polygon Main Angle

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/calculate-polygon-main-angle.html

**id**　93ea409c1bf1896927-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/calculate-polygon-main-angle.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Calculate Polygon Main Angle，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入圖徵 [in_features]、角度欄位 [angle_field]、旋轉方法 （可選） [rotation_method]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

## 從 LAS 資料集移除文件 (資料管理工具) · 用法

**id**　940aa27c393dc05d41-2

**english**　Remove Files From LAS Dataset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- LAS 資料集工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/remove-files-from-las-dataset.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/remove-files-from-las-dataset.html

**text**　目錄路徑必須使用與操作系統相關聯的文件夾分隔符來提供。
帶有顯示金字塔的 LAS 資料集在 2.6 之前的 ArcGIS Pro 版本中不受支持。 移除金字塔將可以在之前的軟件版本中使用 LAS 資料集。 可使用此工具刪除金字塔，以允許這些應用程序使用 LAS 資料集。 但是，首選做法是為這些應用程序創建新的 LAS 資料集，因為生成顯示金字塔的處理時間明顯比創建新的 LAS 資料集的時間長。 可以通過指定現有 LAS 資料集作為輸入，而使用顯示金字塔將文件引用從 LAS 資料集添加到新的 LAS 資料集。 如果要使用其他金字塔點選擇方法，僅考慮刪除顯示金字塔。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/remove-files-from-las-dataset.html#usage
